Rule 3402:
If surface form ?a AND type ProcedureOrInterventionEntity, then type ClinicalProcedure.

Formal Rule:
Head: ?a  type  ClinicalProcedure
Body: ?c  surfaceForm  ?a  ?c  type  ProcedureOrInterventionEntity
